# speccheck/evidence/gdn-floor-probe-2026-10-26.txt — the §10.3 floor probe for the GDN block,
# run over the PINNED dump bytes and the fresh fixtures, which is what turns
# `specification/10-reference-dumps-and-gates.md §10.3` step 1 ("re-run the reference's own F32
# math with its summation re-associated, over the pinned rows, per family") from a requirement
# into a file. The band it wrote is `speccheck/evidence/gdn-band-2026-10-26.json`; the refusals
# that band depends on are witnessed in `gdn-band-loadband-controls-2026-10-26.txt`.
#
# What is in the band, and what is not:
#   * 7 stages floored — `q_hat, k_hat, scan_out, core_attn, state, normed, y` — which is
#     `SCAN_WIDTH_STAGES`, the dataflow closure of the scan. Every band number is
#     `probe max x BAND_FACTORS[key]` (`one_minus_cos` 4 = (1+1)^2, the others 2), re-derived
#     and asserted by `load_band()` on every read. No number in it is typed and none is rounded
#     up "for safety".
#   * 12 stages are NOT floored and are printed by name: they are computed by code the F32 arms
#     and the f64 anchor SHARE, so a floor measured there is 0 by construction — a check that
#     cannot fail. For them the lane claims bit-identity against the pinned reference bytes
#     (the reconciliation table below, 170 rows), never a band.
#   * the population: 178 files hashed (the whole pinned dump, 162,519,680 B, every one
#     sha256-verified before it was measured) x 7 stages x 2 blocks x 26 passes, 102 arm-pass
#     pairs, 714 probe records, 182 anchor rows, 1136 licensed cross-form rows — each printed,
#     parsed back off the record list and compared with a denominator derived from the manifest
#     and the fixture list before anything ran.
#   * the two sabotage directions, on both blocks: 1 ULP on `scan_out` still ACCEPTED (with
#     ndiff 1/4096 proving the comparator saw the perturbation), and a band-exceeding value
#     REJECTED. Printing what the band WOULD have become had the poisoned row been folded in
#     (8.1x / 8.0x looser) is the argument for why the digest check runs before any measurement.
#
# command: /home/erokhins/erokhins_projects/qwen3.5-0.8b/.venv/bin/python speccheck/gdn_ref.py --floor-probe
# rc=0 — FLOOR PROBE PASS. CPU only, torch-CPU, no clock read: this file contains no ms, no
# tok/s and no GB/s figure (`AGENTS.md` §GPU execution).
#
# ---- output ----
# command: speccheck/gdn_ref.py --floor-probe
# floor-probe — torch 2.14.1+cpu CPU threads=8
# dump: data/dumps/2026-10-26-gdn  manifest: checkpoints/dump-manifest-gdn-2026-10-26.json (178 records, 162,519,680 B declared)
# anchor: Form(exact) f64, twin Form(exact_matrix) f64. arms: chunked, substitution, chunked32, recurrent, reassoc + 'pinned' (the pinned bytes themselves — §10.3 step 1's 'the reference's own F32 math, over the pinned rows')
# stages probed: 7 = the scan-width closure ['q_hat', 'k_hat', 'scan_out', 'core_attn', 'state', 'normed', 'y']; 12 of 19 stages are NOT floored here (listed by name at the end, with the reason)
# population: files 178 x stages 7 x blocks 2 x passes 26 (pinned 4 + fixture 9 per block) — records are a sum over passes because arm coverage differs between a pinned and a fresh pass: 102 arm-pass pairs, 714 probe records, 1218 cross-form rows, 182 anchor rows

# The section above is the first run. The command was run a second time, from the pinned bytes again
# with nothing cached between the two, and `speccheck/evidence/gdn-band-2026-10-26.json` came out
# BYTE-IDENTICAL (cmp clean; load_band()'s re-read digest 27a68c7e82c9aa05 in both runs), rc=0, second
# `FLOOR PROBE PASS`. That is the difference between a derived band and a snapshot: the band is a
# function of the pinned bytes and the arm list, so a second run has no way to produce a different
# file short of the bytes or the code changing. Second-run log: tmp/w21_probe_full2.log (tmp/ is
# gitignored; the first run's output is inlined above, which is the part a fresh clone must be able to
# read — pitfalls/60 §self-containment).
